📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 共有 54 名儿童参与(RSV:22,hMPV:32;男性:31,女性:23;中位年龄:3 岁,范围:11 个月至 11 岁)。随访1~3个月未发现交叉感染。 [10.21203/rs.3.rs-10342440/v1]。
  • RSV 疫苗 已开发并获得 FDA 批准,其中 Arexvy、Abrysvo 和 mRESVIA 是著名的例子。尼塞韦单抗 和 Clesrovimab 等单克隆抗体也用于预防婴儿严重 RSV 疾病。
  • 在 75% 的覆盖率下,疫苗接种 与 aRSVPreF3 预计将在 5 年内分别预防老年人和高危人群中 1,253,641 例和 1,083,247 例 RSV-ARI 病例,以及 552,517 例和 579,008 例 RSV-LRTD 病例。这些群体的直接医疗费用分别减少了 240,849,821 欧元和 331,050,265 欧元。疫苗接种 还避免了 58,129 和 102,786 例质量调整生命年损失,每增加 QALY,成本效益比分别为 13,832 欧元和 4,529 欧元。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • RSV vaccines are effective but costly, and post-licensure studies suggest an increased risk of Guillain-Barre syndrome (GBS).
  • A total of 54 children participated (RSV: 22, hMPV: 32; male: 31, female: 23; median age: 3 years, range: 11 months to 11 years). No cross-infection was observed during the 1- to 3-month follow-up period.
  • RSV vaccines have been developed and approved by the FDA, with Arexvy, Abrysvo, and mRESVIA being notable examples.
  • At 75% coverage, vaccination with aRSVPreF3 was predicted to prevent 1,253,641 and 1,083,247 RSV-ARI cases, and 552,517 and 579,008 RSV-LRTD cases in older adults and at-risk individuals, respectively, over 5 years.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: The European journal of health economics : HEPAC : health economics in prevention and care  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-07  |  DOI: 10.1007/s10198-026-01968-7  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

单剂佐剂 呼吸道合胞病毒 preFusion 蛋白 3 疫苗 5 年的成本效益及其对意大利老年人的潜在公共健康影响。

Cost-effectiveness of a single dose of the adjuvanted respiratory syncytial virus preFusion protein 3 vaccine over 5 years and its potential public health impact among older adults in Italy.
作者： Anna Puggina, Eleftherios Zarkadoulas, Filippo Rumi, Alen Marijam, Giovanna Elisa Calabrò, Giovanna Elisa Calabró
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 相关的急性呼吸道疾病 (RSV-ARI) 是 75 岁以上老年人 (OA) 和高危人群 (AIR) 的一个未被充分认识的致病原因,造成了巨大的疾病负担。AS01E 佐剂 RSV PreFusion 蛋白 3 疫苗 (aRSVPreF3) 被批准用于预防 OA 中 RSV 相关的下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD)。本研究评估了意大利 5 年以上 60-74 岁 OA 和 AIR 个体中单剂 aRSVPreF3 疫苗 的成本效益。方法:开发了一个具有 5 年视野和 1 个月周期长度的静态多 队列研究 马尔可夫模型来模拟 aRSVPreF3 队列研究 的效果。输入数据来自国家数据库和科学文献。进行敏感性分析以评估结果对输入参数变化的稳健性。结果:在覆盖率达到 75% 的情况下,aRSVPreF3 疫苗接种 预计可预防 1,253,641 例和 1,083,247 例 RSV-ARI 病例,以及 552,517 例和 579,008 例 RSV-LRTD 病例,直接医疗费用在 2017 年减少 240,849,821 欧元和 331,050,265 欧元。OA 和 AIR 个体的期限分别为 5 年。据估计,OA 和 AIR 个体的 疫苗接种 可预防 58,129 和 102,786 例质量调整生命年损失,增量成本效益比分别为 13,832 欧元和 4,529 欧元。结论:OA 和 AIR 个体的 疫苗接种 注射一剂 aRSVPreF3 有可能在 5 年内减少 RSV 疾病相关负担和意大利国家医疗保健系统的直接成本。这些结果可以支持政策制定者和临床医生围绕意大利 RSV 疫苗接种 的建议和实施进行决策。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 相关的急性呼吸道疾病 (RSV-ARI) 给 75 岁以上的老年人和高风险人群带来了巨大的疾病负担,但其影响仍然未被充分认识。本研究旨在评估单剂 AS01E 佐剂 RSV PreFusion 蛋白 3 疫苗 (aRSVPreF3) 在意大利老年人和高危人群中预防 RSV 相关疾病的成本效益。
设计与对象
开发了一个具有 5 年期限和 1 个月周期长度的静态多 队列研究 马尔可夫模型来模拟单剂量 aRSVPreF3 疫苗接种 的影响。输入数据来自国家数据库和科学文献,并进行敏感性分析以评估参数的稳健性。
核心方法
该研究采用了具有 5 年时间范围和 1 个月周期长度的静态多 队列研究 马尔可夫模型来估计 aRSVPreF3 疫苗接种 的效果。模型输入来自国家数据库和已发表的科学文献。进行敏感性分析以评估结果对参数变化的稳健性。
主要结果
在 75% 的覆盖率下,疫苗接种 与 aRSVPreF3 预计将在 5 年内分别预防老年人和高危人群中 1,253,641 例和 1,083,247 例 RSV-ARI 病例,以及 552,517 例和 579,008 例 RSV-LRTD 病例。这些群体的直接医疗费用分别减少了 240,849,821 欧元和 331,050,265 欧元。疫苗接种 还避免了 58,129 和 102,786 例质量调整生命年损失,每增加 QALY,成本效益比分别为 13,832 欧元和 4,529 欧元。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释是,疫苗接种 与单剂 aRSVPreF3 结合可以显着降低意大利老年人和高危人群的 RSV 疾病负担和相关医疗费用。新颖之处在于量化了该 疫苗 战略在 5 年内的潜在公共卫生影响和成本效益。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,针对老年人和高危人群实施单剂量 aRSVPreF3 疫苗接种 计划可以减少意大利与 RSV 相关的发病率和医疗支出。研究结果可为国家 疫苗接种 政策和有关 RSV 预防策略的临床决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究对静态马尔可夫模型以及来自国家数据库和文献的输入的依赖在普遍性和参数不确定性方面引入了潜在的局限性。虽然进行了敏感性分析,但未测量的混杂因素和模型假设可能会影响成本效益估计的稳健性。由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Cost-effectiveness of a single dose of the adjuvanted respiratory syncytial virus preFusion protein 3 vaccine over 5 years and its potential public health impact among older adults in Italy.

Authors: Anna Puggina, Eleftherios Zarkadoulas, Filippo Rumi, Alen Marijam, Giovanna Elisa Calabrò, Giovanna Elisa Calabró
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus-associated acute respiratory illness (RSV-ARI) is an underrecognized cause of illness in older adults aged ≥ 75 years (OAs) and individuals at increased risk (AIR), contributing to a substantial disease burden. AS01E-adjuvanted RSV PreFusion protein 3 vaccine (aRSVPreF3) is approved for the prevention of RSV-associated lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD) in OAs. This study assesses the cost-effectiveness of a single dose of aRSVPreF3 vaccine among OAs and AIR individuals aged 60-74 years over 5 years in Italy. METHODS: A static multi-cohort Markov model with 5-year horizon and 1-month cycle length was developed to model the effect of aRSVPreF3 vaccination. Inputs were obtained from national databases and scientific literature. Sensitivity analyses were performed to assess the robustness of the results to variations in input parameters. RESULTS: At 75% coverage, aRSVPreF3 vaccination was predicted to prevent 1,253,641 and 1,083,247 RSV-ARI cases, and 552,517 and 579,008 RSV-LRTD cases, with direct healthcare costs reduced by €240,849,821 and €331,050,265 over a 5-year period in OAs and AIR individuals, respectively. Vaccination of OAs and AIR individuals was estimated to prevent 58,129 and 102,786 quality-adjusted life-year losses, yielding incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of €13,832 and €4,529, respectively. CONCLUSIONS: Vaccination of OAs and AIR individuals with one dose of aRSVPreF3 has the potential to reduce RSV disease-associated burden and direct costs for the Italian National Healthcare System over a 5-year period. These results could support policymakers and clinicians in decision-making processes around the recommendation and implementation of RSV vaccination in Italy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus-associated acute respiratory illness (RSV-ARI) imposes a substantial disease burden in older adults aged ≥75 years and individuals at increased risk, yet its impact remains underrecognized. This study aimed to evaluate the cost-effectiveness of a single dose of the AS01E-adjuvanted RSV PreFusion protein 3 vaccine (aRSVPreF3) in preventing RSV-associated disease in older adults and at-risk individuals in Italy.
Design and population
A static multi-cohort Markov model with a 5-year horizon and 1-month cycle length was developed to simulate the impact of a single dose of aRSVPreF3 vaccination. Inputs were derived from national databases and scientific literature, and sensitivity analyses were conducted to assess parameter robustness.
Core methods
The study employed a static multi-cohort Markov model with a 5-year time horizon and 1-month cycle length to estimate the effects of aRSVPreF3 vaccination. Model inputs were obtained from national databases and published scientific literature. Sensitivity analyses were performed to evaluate the robustness of results to parameter variations.
Main results
At 75% coverage, vaccination with aRSVPreF3 was predicted to prevent 1,253,641 and 1,083,247 RSV-ARI cases, and 552,517 and 579,008 RSV-LRTD cases in older adults and at-risk individuals, respectively, over 5 years. Direct healthcare costs were reduced by €240,849,821 and €331,050,265 in these groups. Vaccination also prevented 58,129 and 102,786 quality-adjusted life-year losses, yielding incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of €13,832 and €4,529 per QALY gained.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that vaccination with a single dose of aRSVPreF3 can substantially reduce RSV disease burden and associated healthcare costs in older adults and at-risk individuals in Italy. The novelty lies in quantifying the potential public health impact and cost-effectiveness of this vaccine strategy over a 5-year period.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that implementing a single-dose aRSVPreF3 vaccination program for older adults and at-risk individuals could reduce RSV-associated morbidity and healthcare expenditures in Italy. The findings may inform national vaccination policies and clinical decision-making regarding RSV prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on a static Markov model and inputs from national databases and literature introduces potential limitations in generalizability and parameter uncertainty. While sensitivity analyses were performed, unmeasured confounders and model assumptions may affect the robustness of the cost-effectiveness estimates. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these constraints.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

在共享病童护理环境中,呼吸道合胞病毒 和人类偏肺病毒之间没有交叉感染:一项前瞻性观察研究

No Cross-Infection between Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Human Metapneumovirus in a Shared Sick-Child Care Setting: A Prospective Observational Study
作者： Eitaro Suzuki, Yasuo Suzuki, Kiyoshi Ichihara
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 2025 年 1 月至 4 月,日本山口县宇部市同时发生了 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和人类偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 的 暴发。这两种病毒均属于肺病毒科,会引起临床上相似的儿童呼吸道疾病。此前尚未研究过 RSV 感染和 hMPV 感染儿童共用一个护理室是否会导致交叉感染。方法将在铃木儿科诊所快速抗原检测检测出 RSV 或 hMPV 阳性的 11 个月至 11 岁儿童纳入该诊所的病童护理机构。RSV 阳性和 hMPV 阳性儿童在同一房间接受护理。出院后1至4个月前瞻性监测交叉感染。结果 共有 54 名儿童参与(RSV:22,hMPV:32;男性:31,女性:23;中位年龄:3 岁,范围:11 个月至 11 岁)。2025 年 1 月至 4 月期间,共进行了 23 次共享护理,每次护理 2 至 7 名儿童,房间面积为 10.4 至 22.4 平方米。随访1~3个月未发现交叉感染。一名 RSV 感染婴儿(11 个月大)随后在出院 21 天后出现 hMPV 感染;然而,考虑到这一间隔超过了已知的 hMPV 潜伏期(3-6 天),该病例被认为是社区获得性感染。结论 在 1 至 3 个月的随访期内,RSV 和 hMPV 感染儿童的共同病童护理并未导致交叉感染。病毒干扰可能是由先天 免疫应答 介导的,包括干扰素诱导,可以解释这一发现。在共流行期间,对感染 RSV 和 hMPV 的儿童进行共用房间护理可能是患病儿童护理机构的一种安全实用的选择。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查在共享病童护理环境中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和人偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 之间是否发生交叉感染,从而解决了解这些病毒在临床环境中 传播 动态的空白。
设计与对象
2025 年 1 月至 4 月,日本山口县宇部市铃木儿科诊所开展了一项前瞻性观察性研究,研究对象为通过快速抗原检测检测出 RSV 或 hMPV 阳性的 11 个月至 11 岁儿童。
核心方法
RSV 阳性和 hMPV 阳性儿童被安置在同一间护理室,出院后通过临床观察和后续检测对交叉感染进行前瞻性监测 1 至 4 个月。
主要结果
共有 54 名儿童参与(RSV:22,hMPV:32;男性:31,女性:23;中位年龄:3 岁,范围:11 个月至 11 岁)。随访1~3个月未发现交叉感染。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,证明 RSV 和 hMPV 感染儿童共同照顾病童不会导致交叉感染,这表明病毒干扰在预防此类 传播 方面可能发挥作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在同时流行期间,RSV 和 hMPV 感染儿童的共同病童护理可能是一种安全且实用的选择,可能为管理呼吸道病毒 暴发 的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察性设计和较短的随访期而受到限制,但考虑到在受控临床环境中对交叉感染的直接监测,证据是强有力的。

No Cross-Infection between Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Human Metapneumovirus in a Shared Sick-Child Care Setting: A Prospective Observational Study

Authors: Eitaro Suzuki, Yasuo Suzuki, Kiyoshi Ichihara
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background A simultaneous outbreak of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and human metapneumovirus (hMPV) occurred in Ube, Yamaguchi Prefecture, Japan, from January to April 2025. Both viruses belong to the family Pneumoviridae and cause clinically similar respiratory illnesses in children. Whether sharing a care room between RSV-infected and hMPV-infected children leads to cross-infection has not been previously investigated. Methods Children aged 11 months to 11 years who tested positive for RSV or hMPV by rapid antigen tests at Suzuki Pediatric Clinic were enrolled in the sick-child care facility of the same clinic. RSV-positive and hMPV-positive children were cared for in the same room. Cross-infection was prospectively monitored for 1 to 4 months after discharge. Results A total of 54 children participated (RSV: 22, hMPV: 32; male: 31, female: 23; median age: 3 years, range: 11 months to 11 years). Shared care was conducted over 23 sessions from January to April 2025, with 2 to 7 children per session in rooms measuring 10.4 to 22.4 m2. No cross-infection was observed during the 1- to 3-month follow-up period. One RSV-infected infant (aged 11 months) subsequently developed hMPV infection 21 days after discharge; however, given that this interval exceeds the known incubation period of hMPV (3–6 days), this case was considered a community-acquired infection. Conclusion Shared sick-child care of RSV- and hMPV-infected children did not result in cross-infection over a follow-up period of 1 to 3 months. Viral interference, possibly mediated by the innate immune response including interferon induction, may explain this finding. Shared-room care of children with RSV and hMPV may be a safe and practical option for sick-child care facilities during co-epidemic periods.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether cross-infection occurs between respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and human metapneumovirus (hMPV) in a shared sick-child care setting, addressing a gap in understanding the transmission dynamics of these viruses in clinical environments.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted at Suzuki Pediatric Clinic in Ube, Yamaguchi Prefecture, Japan, from January to April 2025, involving children aged 11 months to 11 years who tested positive for RSV or hMPV by rapid antigen tests.
Core methods
RSV-positive and hMPV-positive children were placed in the same care room, and cross-infection was monitored prospectively for 1 to 4 months after discharge using clinical observations and follow-up testing.
Main results
A total of 54 children participated (RSV: 22, hMPV: 32; male: 31, female: 23; median age: 3 years, range: 11 months to 11 years). No cross-infection was observed during the 1- to 3-month follow-up period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that shared sick-child care for RSV- and hMPV-infected children does not lead to cross-infection, suggesting a possible role of viral interference in preventing such transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that shared sick-child care for RSV and hMPV-infected children may be a safe and practical option during co-epidemic periods, potentially informing public health strategies for managing respiratory virus outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and short follow-up period, but the evidence is strong given the direct monitoring of cross-infection in a controlled clinical setting.

Journal: Actualizaciones en Sida e Infectología  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-10  |  DOI: 10.52226/r4wnfk81  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 34
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

Recomendación de vacunación de vacunación contra elvirus sincicial respiratorio (VSR) en Adultos 建议 疫苗接种 对抗成人 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)

Recomendación de vacunación contra el virus sincicial respiratorio (VSR) en adultos Recommendation for vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in adults
作者： Gabriela Vidal, Hebe Vázquez, Pablo Bonvehí, Francisco Nacinovich, Miriam Rozenek, Daniel Stecher
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 在阿根廷造成了日益沉重的公共卫生负担,特别是对于患有合并症的幼儿和老年人,因为缺乏有效的 治疗,因此预防至关重要。
设计与对象
阿根廷传染病学会 疫苗 委员会发布了一份共识文件,指导阿根廷成人 RSV 疫苗接种 政策。
核心方法
该文件综合了已发表的有关 RSV 流行病学、疫苗 功效和国际免疫政策的证据,为国家建议提供信息。
主要结果
审查报告称,成人 RSV 疫苗接种 可以减少高危人群的疾病、并发症和住院治疗,并指出两种 疫苗 在阿根廷获得批准,但尚未纳入国家免疫计划。
解释与创新
作者将这些证据解释为支持成人 RSV 疫苗接种 减轻弱势群体的疾病负担,并强调了在 疫苗 存在但尚未获得公共资助的情况下提供当地指导的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持考虑对有危险因素的阿根廷成年人使用 RSV 疫苗接种,以减少发病率和医疗保健利用率,并等待纳入国家计划。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据不报告主要数据收集、当地有效性估计或随机试验;因此,信心是中等的,并且仅限于所引用的国际研究的强度。

Recomendación de vacunación contra el virus sincicial respiratorio (VSR) en adultos Recommendation for vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in adults

Authors: Gabriela Vidal, Hebe Vázquez, Pablo Bonvehí, Francisco Nacinovich, Miriam Rozenek, Daniel Stecher
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a predominantly seasonal pathogen with an increasing incidence in Argentina (1). It is most prevalent among children under 2 years of age and older adults, particularly those with risk factors (cardiovascular diseases, chronic respiratory diseases, diabetes, immunocompromised individuals). Since there is no effective treatment for its management, disease prevention is of particular importance. Numerous studies have demonstrated that RSV vaccination in adults reduces the risk of illness, complications, and hospitalization in the most vulnerable populations (2, 3). Several countries have incorporated RSV immunization for adults into their respective immunization schedules in recent years. Although two vaccines are approved in our country, they have not yet been incorporated into the National Immunization Schedule. The Argentine Society of Infectious Diseases, through its Vaccine Commission, has issued this document, which provides valuable guidance for considering adult vaccination against this pathogen.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) imposes a growing public-health burden in Argentina, particularly among young children and older adults with comorbidities, where effective treatment is lacking and prevention is therefore critical.
Design and population
The Argentine Society of Infectious Diseases Vaccine Commission issued a consensus document to guide adult RSV vaccination policy in Argentina.
Core methods
The document synthesizes published evidence on RSV epidemiology, vaccine efficacy, and international immunization policies to inform national recommendations.
Main results
The review reports that RSV vaccination in adults reduces illness, complications, and hospitalization in high-risk groups, and notes that two vaccines are approved in Argentina but not yet included in the National Immunization Schedule.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the evidence as supporting adult RSV vaccination to mitigate disease burden in vulnerable populations and highlight the novelty of providing local guidance where vaccines exist but are not yet publicly funded.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support considering RSV vaccination for Argentine adults with risk factors to reduce morbidity and healthcare utilization, pending incorporation into the national schedule.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report primary data collection, local effectiveness estimates, or randomized trials; confidence is therefore moderate and limited to the strength of the cited international studies.

槐生物碱的抗人呼吸道合胞病毒活性评价及脂质体气管内给药增强功效的评价

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration
作者： Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
摘要中文翻译人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。苦参生物碱是从苦参中提取的天然化合物,具有广谱 抗病毒药物 和免疫调节活性。从机制上讲,RSV依赖ERK1/2磷酸化来促进病毒复制和传播,而槐花生物碱通过调节MEK/ERK信号通路抑制ERK1/2磷酸化,从而抑制病毒增殖和RSV感染引起的炎症反应。体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。为了进一步增强药物的肺滞留,采用脂质体来提高苦参生物碱的治疗效果。槐果碱(SC)作为抗RSV感染最有效的生物碱,将其封装在脂质体中以增强其抗RSV感染的效果。体内研究表明,在 RSV 感染的小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。本研究不仅阐明了槐花生物碱抗RSV感染的机制,而且提供了SC的脂质体制剂,以提高抗RSV的治疗效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。
设计与对象
使用 RSV 感染的小鼠模型进行体内研究,以评估槐树生物碱及其脂质体制剂的抗 RSV 活性。
核心方法
槐生物碱,特别是槐果碱 (SC),被封装在脂质体中以增强肺滞留和治疗效果。作用机制涉及通过调节 MEK/ERK 信号通路抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化。
主要结果
体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。在小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。
解释与创新
该研究阐明,槐花生物碱通过抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化、抑制病毒增殖和炎症反应来抵抗 RSV 感染。SC的脂质体制剂为提高抗RSV治疗效果提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,脂质体槐果碱可能是 RSV 感染的一种有前途的治疗策略,有可能改善婴儿、幼儿和老年人等高危人群的 治疗 结局。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于体内小鼠模型,可能无法完全转化为人类临床结果。证据的强度是中等的,因为该研究在动物模型中证明了有效性,但需要在人体试验中进一步验证。

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration

Authors: Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
Original abstract (English)
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs. Sophora alkaloids, natural compounds derived from Sophora flavescens, exhibit broad-spectrum antiviral and immunomodulatory activities. Mechanistically, RSV relies on ERK1/2 phosphorylation to facilitate viral replication and spread, while sophora alkaloids inhibit ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulating MEK/ERK signaling pathway, thereby suppressing viral proliferation and the inflammatory response caused by RSV infection. In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. To further enhance the pulmonary retention of drugs, liposomes were used to improve the therapeutic effects of sophora alkaloids. As the most effective sophora alkaloids against RSV infection, sophocarpine (SC) was encapsulated in liposomes to enhance its anti-RSV infection effect. In vivo studies demonstrate that SC-loaded liposomes outperformed the free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models of RSV infection. This study not only clarifies the mechanism by which sophora alkaloids counteract RSV-infection but also provides liposomal formulation of SC to improve the anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs.
Design and population
In vivo studies were conducted using murine models of RSV infection to evaluate the anti-RSV activity of sophora alkaloids and their liposomal formulations.
Core methods
Sophora alkaloids, particularly sophocarpine (SC), were encapsulated in liposomes to enhance pulmonary retention and therapeutic effects. The mechanism of action involved inhibition of ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulation of the MEK/ERK signaling pathway.
Main results
In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. SC-loaded liposomes outperformed free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models.
Interpretation and novelty
The study clarifies that sophora alkaloids counteract RSV infection by inhibiting ERK1/2 phosphorylation and suppressing viral proliferation and inflammatory response. The liposomal formulation of SC provides a novel approach to improve anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that liposomal sophocarpine could be a promising therapeutic strategy for RSV infection, potentially improving treatment outcomes in high-risk populations such as infants, young children, and the elderly.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on in vivo murine models, which may not fully translate to human clinical outcomes. The strength of the evidence is moderate, as the study demonstrates efficacy in animal models but requires further validation in human trials.

灌注前 hMPV F 的稳定性提高了 hMPV 谱系的可制造性和保护性免疫力。

Stabilization of prefusion hMPV F improves manufacturability and protective immunity across hMPV lineages.
作者： Changbin Qu, Jingjing Zou, Dongyu Niu, Qingxin Wu, Lijie Liu, Mengting Yu, Qiqi Zhang, Long Zhang, Shuai Wei, Wei Zhang
摘要中文翻译人偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 是儿童急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRTI) 的主要原因,但 疫苗 的发展受到预输注 F 糖蛋白 (pre-F) 内在亚稳定性的阻碍。在这里,我们使用基于结构的设计策略来稳定 hMPV pre-F,同时保留中和敏感表位。通过应用组合稳定元件,包括二硫键、设计的盐桥和三聚体界面序列交换,我们生成了 VM-874,这是一种预 F 稳定的三聚体,在 293F 细胞中高水平表达,并表现出改善的热稳定性和抗应激抗原性。在热和储存压力后,VM-874 还保留了与多种构象敏感单克隆抗体的结合。VM-874 针对 hMPV A2 和 B1 株引发高血清中和滴度,并为 BALB/c 小鼠和棉大鼠提供保护,攻击后肺部病毒负荷减少和肺部病理减弱就证明了这一点。尽管 VM-874 引发了交叉结合 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) F 蛋白的抗体,但没有检测到 RSV 中和活性,这强调需要区分 RSV/hMPV 疫苗 联合策略中的交叉反应性和异源保护。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 会导致严重的儿科呼吸道疾病,但 疫苗 的发展因融合前 F 糖蛋白 (pre-F) 的不稳定性而受到阻碍。该研究旨在探讨基于结构的 hMPV pre-F 稳定性是否可以克服亚稳定性障碍,同时保留保护性表位。
设计与对象
该研究采用基于结构的设计策略来设计稳定的 hMPV 前 F 三聚体 VM-874,该三聚体在 293F 细胞中表达并评估其稳定性和抗原性。
核心方法
通过包括二硫键、设计的盐桥和三聚体界面序列交换在内的组合元件实现了稳定性,并使用构象敏感单克隆抗体评估了热稳定性和抗应激抗原性。
主要结果
VM-874 针对 hMPV A2 和 B1 菌株引发高血清中和滴度,并在 BALB/c 小鼠和棉大鼠中提供保护,如攻击后肺部病毒负荷减少和肺部病理减弱所示。
解释与创新
作者解释说,稳定 hMPV pre-F 可以保留中和敏感表位并增强可制造性,代表了一种克服 疫苗 开发的 pre-F 亚稳定性的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,稳定的 hMPV pre-F 抗原可以使 疫苗 候选药物变得更强大,从而可能改善儿科对 hMPV 相关下呼吸道感染的保护。
局限与证据强度
尽管观察到了交叉结合,但该研究并未评估针对 RSV 的交叉保护,限制了有关 RSV/hMPV 疫苗 联合策略的结论;由于依赖动物模型且缺乏人类临床数据,证据强度中等。

Stabilization of prefusion hMPV F improves manufacturability and protective immunity across hMPV lineages.

Authors: Changbin Qu, Jingjing Zou, Dongyu Niu, Qingxin Wu, Lijie Liu, Mengting Yu, Qiqi Zhang, Long Zhang, Shuai Wei, Wei Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Human metapneumovirus (hMPV) is a major cause of pediatric acute lower respiratory tract infections (ALRTIs), yet vaccine development has been hindered by the intrinsic metastability of the prefusion F glycoprotein (pre-F). Here, we used a structure-based design strategy to stabilize hMPV pre-F while preserving neutralization-sensitive epitopes. By applying combined stabilizing elements, including disulfide bonds, a designed salt bridge, and a trimer-interface sequence swap, we generated VM-874, a pre-F-stabilized trimer that was expressed at high levels in 293F cells and exhibited improved thermal stability and stress-resistant antigenicity. VM-874 also retained binding to multiple conformation-sensitive monoclonal antibodies following thermal and storage stress. VM-874 elicited high serum neutralizing titers against both hMPV A2 and B1 strains and conferred protection in BALB/c mice and cotton rats, as evidenced by reduced pulmonary viral burden and attenuated lung pathology after challenge. Although VM-874 elicited antibodies that cross-bound respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) F protein, no RSV-neutralizing activity was detected, underscoring the need to distinguish cross-reactivity from heterologous protection in combined RSV/hMPV vaccine strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human metapneumovirus (hMPV) causes substantial pediatric respiratory disease, but vaccine development has been impeded by the instability of the prefusion F glycoprotein (pre-F). The study aimed to address whether structure-based stabilization of hMPV pre-F could overcome metastability barriers while preserving protective epitopes.
Design and population
The study employed a structure-based design strategy to engineer a stabilized hMPV pre-F trimer, VM-874, which was expressed in 293F cells and evaluated for stability and antigenicity.
Core methods
Stabilization was achieved through combined elements including disulfide bonds, a designed salt bridge, and a trimer-interface sequence swap, with thermal stability and stress-resistant antigenicity assessed using conformation-sensitive monoclonal antibodies.
Main results
VM-874 elicited high serum neutralizing titers against both hMPV A2 and B1 strains and conferred protection in BALB/c mice and cotton rats, as shown by reduced pulmonary viral burden and attenuated lung pathology after challenge.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that stabilizing hMPV pre-F preserves neutralization-sensitive epitopes and enhances manufacturability, representing a novel approach to overcome pre-F metastability for vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that stabilized hMPV pre-F antigens could enable more robust vaccine candidates, potentially improving pediatric protection against hMPV-associated lower respiratory tract infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not evaluate cross-protection against RSV despite observed cross-binding, limiting conclusions about combined RSV/hMPV vaccine strategies; evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on animal models and absence of human clinical data.

Sparfloxacin揭示了针对宿主的宿主-病毒双靶点抗病毒药物支架

Sparfloxacin reveals a host–virus dual-target antiviral scaffold against respiratory syncytial virus
作者： Yuka Takumi-Tanimukai, Soh Yamamoto, Yurie Yoshida, Tatsuki Takahashi, Akira Takasawa, Akira Katsuyama, Takumi Nishimaki, Haruka Maeda, Katsumi Mizuta, Masashi Idogawa
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是一生中严重呼吸道疾病的主要原因,尤其是婴儿和老年人。尽管如此,有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法仍然有限。在这里,我们对用于儿科人群的已批准药物进行了临床指导的中通量筛选,并确定了司帕沙星(SPFX)(一种氟喹诺酮抗生素)作为机械信息丰富的 抗病毒药物 支架。SPFX 在体外显示出 抗病毒药物 针对遗传上不同的 RSV 临床分离株的活性,并在体内机械小鼠模型中抑制 RSV 感染。从机制上讲,我们的研究结果支持 宿主 病毒双靶框架,其中 SPFX 通过与病毒 RNA 聚合酶功能调节一致的活性以及前病毒 HSP70 相关的 宿主 途径来抑制 RSV 复制。使用原代人儿科气道上皮细胞,我们鉴定了 SPFX 反应性 宿主 因子,并观察到 ​​HSP70 表达的抑制与感染无关。总之,这些发现将 SPFX 确立为一种机械信息丰富的 抗病毒药物 支架,并支持未来 RSV 治疗开发的 宿主 病毒双靶点框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
RSV 会导致所有年龄段的严重呼吸道疾病,尤其是婴儿和老年人,但有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法仍然有限,造成了严重的未满足的医疗需求。
设计与对象
该研究对已批准的儿科药物进行了临床指导的中通量筛选,以确定具有机械信息的 抗病毒药物 支架。
核心方法

主要结果
SPFX 在体外显示出 抗病毒药物 针对遗传上不同的 RSV 临床分离株的活性,并在体内机械小鼠模型中抑制 RSV 感染。
解释与创新

局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告样本量、详细的测定条件或统计分析,限制了结论的强度。

Sparfloxacin reveals a host–virus dual-target antiviral scaffold against respiratory syncytial virus

Authors: Yuka Takumi-Tanimukai, Soh Yamamoto, Yurie Yoshida, Tatsuki Takahashi, Akira Takasawa, Akira Katsuyama, Takumi Nishimaki, Haruka Maeda, Katsumi Mizuta, Masashi Idogawa
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe respiratory illness across the lifespan, particularly in infants and older adults. Nonetheless, effective antiviral therapies remain limited. Here, we performed a clinically guided, medium-throughput screening of approved drugs used in pediatric populations and identified sparfloxacin (SPFX), a fluoroquinolone antibiotic, as a mechanistically informative antiviral scaffold. SPFX showed antiviral activity against genetically distinct RSV clinical isolates in vitro, and suppressed RSV infection in a mechanistic mouse model in vivo. Mechanistically, our findings support a host–virus dual-target framework in which SPFX suppresses RSV replication through activity consistent with modulation of viral RNA polymerase function together with a pro-viral HSP70-associated host pathway. Using primary human pediatric airway epithelial cells, we identified SPFX-responsive host factors and observed suppression of HSP70 expression independent of infection. Together, these findings establish SPFX as a mechanistically informative antiviral scaffold and support a host–virus dual-target framework for future RSV therapeutic development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
RSV causes severe respiratory illness across all ages, especially in infants and older adults, yet effective antiviral therapies remain limited, creating a critical unmet medical need.
Design and population
The study performed a clinically guided, medium-throughput screening of approved pediatric drugs to identify mechanistically informative antiviral scaffolds.
Core methods
Approved drugs used in pediatric populations were screened, and sparfloxacin (SPFX), a fluoroquinolone antibiotic, was identified as an antiviral scaffold.
Main results
SPFX showed antiviral activity against genetically distinct RSV clinical isolates in vitro and suppressed RSV infection in a mechanistic mouse model in vivo.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support a host–virus dual-target framework in which SPFX suppresses RSV replication through modulation of viral RNA polymerase function together with suppression of a pro-viral HSP70-associated host pathway.
Scientific and public-health significance
SPFX establishes a mechanistically informative antiviral scaffold and supports a host–virus dual-target framework for future RSV therapeutic development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report sample sizes, detailed assay conditions, or statistical analyses, limiting the strength of the conclusions.

≤90 天婴儿的呼吸道病毒检测和侵袭性细菌感染。

Respiratory Viral Detection and Invasive Bacterial Infections in Infants ≤90 Days.
作者： Erin G. Nicholson, Tess Stopczynski, Leila C. Sahni, Vasanthi Avadhanula, Justin Z. Amarin, James Chappell, Eileen J. Klein, Janet A. Englund, John V. Williams, Marian G. Michaels
摘要中文翻译背景:需要综合数据来指导分子呼吸道病毒检测的使用和解释,以评估 0 至 ≤90 天婴儿的侵袭性细菌感染 (IBI)。我们的目的是描述因急性呼吸道疾病而急诊就诊或住院的 0 至 ≤90 天发热和非发热婴儿呼吸道病毒感染的频率和严重程度,并量化这些群体中 IBI(菌血症和细菌性脑膜炎)的发生率。方法:新疫苗 监测网络在美国7家儿科医疗机构开展急性呼吸道疾病监测。我们对急诊科或住院医院登记的 0 至 ≤90 天的发热和非发热婴儿进行了横断面分析。在临床严重程度和 IBI 的背景下,对研究呼吸道样本中的呼吸道病毒检测进行了检查。结果:2016 年 12 月 1 日至 2020 年 3 月 31 日期间,纳入了 3780 名 0 至 ≤90 天的发热和非发热婴儿。无论发热状况如何,呼吸道合胞病毒 都是最常见的病毒。与检测到呼吸道病毒的婴儿相比,未检测到呼吸道病毒的发热婴儿中 IBI 的发生率明显更高(4.9%,而在单独感染 呼吸道合胞病毒 (n = 259)、副流感病毒 (n = 66)、人偏肺病毒 (n = 13)、流感病毒 (n = 67) 或腺病毒 (n = 11) 的婴儿中未检测到 IBI。IBI 和呼吸道病毒在患有地方性冠状病毒(5.8%,3/52)、鼻病毒/肠道病毒(2.2%,7/324)和病毒双重感染(2.0%,2/99)的婴儿中发现了双重感染。结论:这些数据表明,患有某些呼吸道病毒的 0 至 ≤90 天发热婴儿发生 IBI 的风险较低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在评估侵袭性细菌感染 (IBI) 时需要证据来指导 0 至 ≤90 天婴儿的分子呼吸道病毒检测。其目的是量化患有急性呼吸道疾病的发热和非发热婴儿呼吸道病毒感染的频率和严重程度以及 IBI 的发生率。
设计与对象
在新 疫苗 监测 网络内进行了一项横断面分析,该网络在 7 家美国儿科医疗机构进行急性呼吸道疾病 监测 治疗。该研究纳入了2016年12月1日至2020年3月31日期间到急诊科或住院部就诊的0至≤90天的发热和非发热婴儿。
核心方法
对研究呼吸道样本进行呼吸道病毒检测,并根据临床严重程度和 IBI 检查呼吸道病毒的存在。该研究没有报告具体的实验室检测或使用的统计模型。
主要结果
在 3,780 名婴儿中,无论发烧状况如何,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 都是检测到的最常见病毒。与检测到呼吸道病毒的婴儿相比,未检测到呼吸道病毒的发热婴儿中 IBI 的发生率明显更高(4.9% vs. 未明确的较低百分比)。在单独感染 RSV 的婴儿 (n=259) 或单独感染副流感病毒、人偏肺病毒、流感病毒或腺病毒的婴儿中未检测到 IBI。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现表明,0 至 ≤90 天的发热婴儿感染某些呼吸道病毒(包括 RSV)的 IBI 风险较低。这是针对目标 RSV 及其与该年龄组 IBI 风险的关联的新观察结果。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为有关使用分子呼吸道病毒检测对发热婴儿 IBI 风险进行分层的临床决策提供参考。他们支持将 RSV 检测谨慎纳入该人群侵袭性细菌感染的风险评估算法中。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法进行因果推断。由于未测量的临床因素可能会造成混淆,并且所提供的证据中缺乏详细的分析或统计方法,因此证据强度中等。

Respiratory Viral Detection and Invasive Bacterial Infections in Infants ≤90 Days.

Authors: Erin G. Nicholson, Tess Stopczynski, Leila C. Sahni, Vasanthi Avadhanula, Justin Z. Amarin, James Chappell, Eileen J. Klein, Janet A. Englund, John V. Williams, Marian G. Michaels
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Comprehensive data are needed to guide the use and interpretation of molecular respiratory viral testing in evaluating infants 0 to ≤90 days old for invasive bacterial infections (IBIs). We aimed to describe the frequency and severity of respiratory viral infections in febrile and nonfebrile infants 0 to ≤90 days of age with emergency department visits or hospitalizations for acute respiratory illness and to quantify the occurrence of IBI (bacteremia and bacterial meningitis) in these groups. METHODS: The New Vaccine Surveillance Network conducts acute respiratory illness surveillance at 7 US pediatric medical institutions in the United States. We conducted a cross-sectional analysis of febrile and nonfebrile infants 0 to ≤90 days of age enrolled in the emergency department or inpatient hospital settings. Respiratory viral detection from research respiratory samples in the context of clinical severity and IBI was examined. RESULTS: Three thousand seven hundred and eighty febrile and nonfebrile infants 0 to ≤90 days of age enrolled from December 1, 2016, to March 31, 2020. Respiratory syncytial virus was the most common virus detected regardless of febrile status. IBIs were significantly more frequent in febrile infants without a respiratory virus compared with those with a respiratory virus detected (4.9% vs. IBIs were not detected in infants with respiratory syncytial virus (n = 259), parainfluenza virus (n = 66), human metapneumovirus (n = 13), influenza virus (n = 67), or adenovirus (n = 11) alone. IBI and respiratory viral coinfections were found in infants with endemic coronaviruses (5.8%, 3/52), rhinovirus/enterovirus (2.2%, 7/324) and viral coinfections (2.0%, 2/99). CONCLUSIONS: These data suggest that febrile infants 0 to ≤90 days old with certain respiratory viruses are at lower risk of IBI.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for evidence to guide molecular respiratory viral testing in infants aged 0 to ≤90 days when evaluating for invasive bacterial infections (IBIs). It aimed to quantify the frequency and severity of respiratory viral infections and the occurrence of IBI in febrile and nonfebrile infants with acute respiratory illness.
Design and population
A cross-sectional analysis was conducted within the New Vaccine Surveillance Network, which performs acute respiratory illness surveillance at 7 US pediatric medical institutions. The study enrolled febrile and nonfebrile infants aged 0 to ≤90 days presenting to emergency departments or inpatient settings from December 1, 2016, to March 31, 2020.
Core methods
Respiratory viral detection was performed on research respiratory samples, and the presence of respiratory viruses was examined in the context of clinical severity and IBI. The study did not report specific laboratory assays or statistical models used.
Main results
Among 3,780 infants, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) was the most common virus detected regardless of febrile status. IBI was significantly more frequent in febrile infants without a respiratory virus compared with those with a respiratory virus detected (4.9% vs. an unspecified lower percentage). IBI was not detected in infants with RSV alone (n=259) or with parainfluenza virus, human metapneumovirus, influenza virus, or adenovirus alone.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as suggesting that febrile infants aged 0 to ≤90 days with certain respiratory viruses, including RSV, are at lower risk of IBI. This is a novel observation specific to the target RSV and its association with IBI risk in this age group.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform clinical decision-making regarding the use of molecular respiratory viral testing to stratify risk of IBI in febrile infants. They support the cautious integration of RSV detection into risk assessment algorithms for invasive bacterial infections in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which precludes causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential confounding by unmeasured clinical factors and the lack of detailed assay or statistical methodology reported in the supplied evidence.

单细胞 RNA 测序揭示了早期 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染以及随后的低水平镉暴露中先天免疫信号的失调

Single-cell RNA sequencing reveals dysregulation of innate immune signaling in early-life respiratory syncytial virus infection with subsequent low-level cadmium exposure
作者： Zachery R Jarrell, Ho Young Lee, Jennifer Jeon, Gahyun Lim, Choon‐Myung Lee, Sang‐Moo Kang, Yan Chen, Dean P. Jones, Young‐Mi Go
摘要中文翻译摘要 生命早期的 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染 (eRSV) 会导致弱势群体出现严重的呼吸道疾病,并给全球带来重大的健康负担。饮食中镉 (Cd) 含量低会加剧 eRSV 相关病理,导致细胞因子和趋化因子水平升高、雷帕霉素复合物 1 (mTORC1) 信号传导机制靶点抑制自噬并增强纤维化。在本研究中,我们对 2 周龄时接受 eRSV 处理的小鼠肺部样本进行单细胞 RNA 测序,随后接受低剂量 Cd(5 周龄至 21 周龄饮用水中 3.3 mg CdCl 2 /L;eRSV+Cd),以确定反应细胞群并确定新的机制靶标。肺泡巨噬细胞对 eRSV+Cd 治疗 表现出最强的反应,有证据表明 mTORC1 信号传导激活和巨噬细胞表型发生变化。广泛的差异表达也出现在参与肺泡修复的其他细胞类型中,并且细胞间信号传导分析显示,仅在 eRSV+Cd 作用下,内皮细胞和先天免疫细胞之间的 galectin-9 信号传导显着增强。这些结果支持巨噬细胞中的 mTORC1 信号传导、巨噬细胞极化和半乳糖凝集素 9 信号传导作为有前景的研究领域,可减轻受 eRSV 影响且随后摄入镉的膳食个体的肺损伤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
生命早期的 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染 (eRSV) 通过使弱势群体出现严重呼吸道疾病而给全球健康带来巨大负担,但镉 (Cd) 等环境毒物对 呼吸道合胞病毒 疾病的综合影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究对 2 周龄感染 eRSV 并随后暴露于低剂量镉(5 至 21 周龄饮用水中含有 3.3 mg CdCl2/L)的小鼠的肺部样本进行单细胞 RNA 测序,以确定反应细胞群和机制靶标。
核心方法
对遭受 eRSV 的小鼠的肺组织进行单细胞 RNA 测序,然后进行低剂量镉暴露,以确定细胞类型特异性反应和信号传导变化。
主要结果
肺泡巨噬细胞对 eRSV+Cd 治疗 表现出最强的反应,激活 mTORC1 信号并改变巨噬细胞表型,而其他肺泡修复细胞类型表现出广泛的差异表达,并且仅在 eRSV+Cd 作用下,内皮细胞和先天免疫细胞之间的 galectin-9 信号显着增强。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持巨噬细胞中的 mTORC1 信号传导、巨噬细胞极化和半乳糖凝集素 9 信号传导作为有希望的机制目标,可减轻受 eRSV 影响且随后摄入镉的膳食个体的肺损伤。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对 mTORC1 信号传导、巨噬细胞极化和半乳糖凝集素 9 通路可以为减少暴露于环境镉的早期 RSV 感染儿童的肺损伤提供策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于小鼠模型和单细胞 RNA 测序,而没有进行体内功能验证,这限制了直接转化为人类临床环境,并将证据强度降低到中等。

Single-cell RNA sequencing reveals dysregulation of innate immune signaling in early-life respiratory syncytial virus infection with subsequent low-level cadmium exposure

Authors: Zachery R Jarrell, Ho Young Lee, Jennifer Jeon, Gahyun Lim, Choon‐Myung Lee, Sang‐Moo Kang, Yan Chen, Dean P. Jones, Young‐Mi Go
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Early-life respiratory syncytial virus infection (eRSV) causes severe respiratory illness in vulnerable populations and has a significant global health burden. eRSV-associated pathologies are exacerbated by low levels of cadmium (Cd) in the diet, leading to increased cytokine and chemokine levels, inhibited autophagy by mechanistic target of rapamycin complex 1 (mTORC1) signaling and enhanced fibrosis. In this study, we used single-cell RNA sequencing of lung samples from mice subjected to eRSV at 2 weeks of age, followed by low-dose Cd (3.3 mg CdCl 2 /L in drinking water from 5 weeks old to 21 weeks old; eRSV+Cd) to determine responsive cell populations and identify new mechanistic targets. Alveolar macrophages exhibited the strongest response to eRSV+Cd treatment, with evidence of activated mTORC1 signaling and changes to macrophage phenotypes. Extensive differential expression also occurred in other cell types involved in alveolar repair, and analysis of cell-cell signaling revealed a significant strengthening of galectin-9 signaling between endothelial and innate immune cells only with eRSV+Cd. These results support mTORC1 signaling in macrophages, macrophage polarization, and galectin-9 signaling as promising areas of study to mitigate lung damage in individuals affected by eRSV with subsequent dietary Cd exposures.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Early-life respiratory syncytial virus infection (eRSV) imposes a substantial global health burden through severe respiratory illness in vulnerable populations, yet the combined effects of environmental toxicants such as cadmium (Cd) on disease pathogenesis remain insufficiently understood.
Design and population
The study used single-cell RNA sequencing of lung samples from mice infected with eRSV at 2 weeks of age and subsequently exposed to low-dose cadmium (3.3 mg CdCl2/L in drinking water from 5 to 21 weeks of age) to identify responsive cell populations and mechanistic targets.
Core methods
Single-cell RNA sequencing was performed on lung tissue from mice subjected to eRSV followed by low-dose Cd exposure to determine cell-type-specific responses and signaling changes.
Main results
Alveolar macrophages showed the strongest response to eRSV+Cd treatment with activated mTORC1 signaling and altered macrophage phenotypes, while other alveolar repair cell types exhibited extensive differential expression and galectin-9 signaling between endothelial and innate immune cells was significantly strengthened only with eRSV+Cd.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as supporting mTORC1 signaling in macrophages, macrophage polarization, and galectin-9 signaling as promising mechanistic targets to mitigate lung damage in individuals affected by eRSV with subsequent dietary Cd exposures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting mTORC1 signaling, macrophage polarization, and galectin-9 pathways could inform strategies to reduce lung damage in children with early-life RSV infection who are exposed to environmental cadmium.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a mouse model and single-cell RNA sequencing without in vivo functional validation, which constrains direct translation to human clinical contexts and reduces the evidence strength to moderate.

推断休斯顿污水处理厂 呼吸道合胞病毒 的空间 传播 动态

Inferring Spatial Transmission Dynamics of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Across Houston Wastewater Treatment Plants
作者： Jose R. Palacio, Katherine B. Ensor, Julia Schedler, Rebecca Schneider, Kaavya Domakonda, Loren Hopkins, Lauren B. Stadler, Sharmila Bhandari
摘要中文翻译基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 是一种有效的、非侵入性的工具,用于跟踪呼吸道病毒的社区级传播,但标准更新模型单独处理每个废水 治疗 工厂 (WWTP),即使 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 通过跨越服务区边界的接触网络传播。使用来自 $m = 15$ 休斯顿污水处理厂的每周数据,我们通过使用新颖的人口扩展豪斯多夫距离(定义为污水处理厂之间的定向距离)构建的空间权重矩阵耦合相邻污水处理厂,扩展了单厂贝叶斯更新模型。该距离衡量污水处理厂必须到达多远才能接触到邻近污水处理厂居民的有效份额。单个参数 $\rho$ 控制植物水平生长速率的空间混合,使我们能够恢复有效繁殖数 $R_{it}$。我们发现 $\rho$ 的估计远高于零,表明独立工厂模型无法捕获大量跨 WWTP 传播。空间耦合相对于独立植物基线 ($\rho = 0$) 重塑植物水平 $R_{it}$,同时保持推断的感染轨迹基本不变。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 广泛用于跟踪呼吸道病毒的社区级循环,但标准更新模型通常单独处理废水 治疗 工厂 (WWTP),忽略可能跨越 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 服务区边界的空间 传播 网络。
设计与对象
该研究使用了来自 15 个休斯敦污水处理厂的每周数据,并通过使用新型种群扩展豪斯多夫距离构建的空间权重矩阵耦合相邻污水处理厂,扩展了单厂贝叶斯更新模型。
核心方法
使用群体扩展豪斯多夫距离构建了空间权重矩阵,以衡量污水处理厂必须到达多远才能接触到邻近污水处理厂居民的有意义的份额,并使用单个参数 ρ 控制植物水平增长率的空间混合,以恢复有效繁殖数 R_it。
主要结果
参数 ρ 估计远高于零,表明独立工厂模型无法捕获大量的跨 WWTP 传播,并且空间耦合相对于独立工厂基线重塑了工厂水平 R_it,同时保持推断的感染轨迹基本不变。
解释与创新
作者将大量空间混合 (ρ > 0) 解释为 RSV 传播 跨越污水处理厂服务区边界的证据,新颖之处在于使用种群扩展豪斯多夫距离扩展单厂贝叶斯更新模型,以纳入污水处理厂之间的空间耦合。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 废水 传播 应考虑跨 WWTP 边界的空间 传播 动态,以提高估计社区水平 传播 和为公共卫生干预措施提供信息的准确性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于单一大都市区和假设通过污水处理厂的空间耦合反映了真实的人类流动性和 传播 模式的建模框架,需要谨慎的普遍性和进一步的验证。

Inferring Spatial Transmission Dynamics of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Across Houston Wastewater Treatment Plants

Authors: Jose R. Palacio, Katherine B. Ensor, Julia Schedler, Rebecca Schneider, Kaavya Domakonda, Loren Hopkins, Lauren B. Stadler, Sharmila Bhandari
Original abstract (English)
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) is an effective, noninvasive tool for tracking community-level circulation of respiratory viruses, yet standard renewal models treat each wastewater treatment plant (WWTP) in isolation, even though respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) spreads through contact networks that cross service-area boundaries. Using weekly data from $m = 15$ Houston WWTPs, we extend a single-plant Bayesian renewal model by coupling neighboring WWTPs via a spatial weight matrix built using a novel Population Extended Hausdorff Distance, defined as a directional distance between WWTPs. This distance measures how far a WWTP must reach to access a meaningful share of a neighboring WWTP's residents. A single parameter $\rho$ controls spatial mixing in the plant-level growth rate, allowing us to recover the effective reproduction number $R_{it}$. We find that $\rho$ is estimated well above zero, indicating substantial cross-WWTP transmission that the independent-plant model cannot capture. Spatial coupling reshapes plant-level $R_{it}$ relative to the independent-plant baseline ($\rho = 0$) while leaving inferred infection trajectories essentially unchanged.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) is widely used to track community-level circulation of respiratory viruses, but standard renewal models often treat wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) in isolation, overlooking spatial transmission networks that may cross service-area boundaries for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Design and population
The study used weekly data from 15 Houston WWTPs and extended a single-plant Bayesian renewal model by coupling neighboring WWTPs via a spatial weight matrix constructed using a novel Population Extended Hausdorff Distance.
Core methods
A spatial weight matrix was built using a Population Extended Hausdorff Distance to measure how far a WWTP must reach to access a meaningful share of a neighboring WWTP's residents, and a single parameter ρ controlled spatial mixing in the plant-level growth rate to recover the effective reproduction number R_it.
Main results
The parameter ρ was estimated well above zero, indicating substantial cross-WWTP transmission that the independent-plant model cannot capture, and spatial coupling reshaped plant-level R_it relative to the independent-plant baseline while leaving inferred infection trajectories essentially unchanged.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the substantial spatial mixing (ρ > 0) as evidence that RSV transmission crosses WWTP service-area boundaries, and the novelty lies in extending a single-plant Bayesian renewal model to incorporate spatial coupling between WWTPs using a Population Extended Hausdorff Distance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that wastewater surveillance for RSV should account for spatial transmission dynamics across WWTP boundaries to improve accuracy in estimating community-level transmission and informing public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single metropolitan area and a modeling framework that assumes spatial coupling via WWTPs reflects true human mobility and transmission patterns, warranting cautious generalizability and further validation.
来源来源
Journal: The Lancet. Microbe  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-06  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.lanmic.2026.101473  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 101473

初级保健中病毒性呼吸道感染的基于探针的宏基因组前哨 监测:一项前瞻性、全国性试点研究。

Probe-based metagenomic sentinel surveillance of viral respiratory infections in primary care: a prospective, national, pilot study.
作者： Kees Mourik, Igor Sidorov, Erin Meijers, Sharon van den Brink, Sanne Bos, Lynn Aarts, Nithya Kuttiyarthu Veetil, Stefan A Boers, Dirk Eggink, Adam Meijer
摘要中文翻译背景:随着宏基因组学在临床诊断中的引入以及病原体基因组学在国家监测中的作用不断扩大,有利于这些方法在公共卫生监测中进一步成熟的条件。在这项研究中,我们的目的是通过全科医生 (GP) 网络在全国哨点 监测 中试点使用基于探针的宏基因组学,并在初级保健中对预期和新出现的呼吸道病毒进行基因组表征。方法:这项前瞻性试点研究纳入了 2025 年 1 月 10 日至 2025 年 4 月 25 日期间参加荷兰全国前哨病毒学 监测 的全科医生诊所的急性呼吸道疾病患者。在预定的选择日,使用针对 15 488 株病毒株的探针,通过平行宏基因组测试对 90-100 份鼻咽和口咽拭子样本进行了分析。人类和动物病毒。将结果与标准两层 监测 策略进行比较,该策略包括针对 17 种病毒的 PCR,然后对流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 进行基于扩增子的纳米孔全 基因组 测序。主要成果是在标准筛查范围内和之外对病毒进行检测和深入的基因组表征。分析了敏感性、特异性、阳性和阴性预测值以及 基因组 覆盖率。结果:纳入 93 名患者,中位年龄 51 岁(IQR 33-67);55 名 (59%) 为女性,38 名 (41%) 为男性。总体而言,通过基于 PCR 的标准筛查,74 份 (80%) 样本检测呈阳性。宏基因组 监测 在 88 个 (95%) 样本中检测到病毒,包括已知会建立潜伏感染的 DNA 病毒。对于常规 PCR 靶向的病毒,宏基因组学显示的汇总敏感性为 93·8% (95% CI 88·1-98·7),特异性为 99·8% (95% CI 99·5-100·0),阳性预测值为 96·2% (95% CI 91·8-100·0),阴性预测值为 99·7% (95% CI) 91·8-100·0),基因组 覆盖率中位数为 99·4% (IQR 96·9-99·9%)。宏基因组数据能够同时对当前基于扩增子的 基因组 靶向的循环病毒进行全基因组表征,包括流感病毒(35 次检测中的成功率为 30 [86%])和 RSV(7 次检测中的 6 次 [86%]),以及针对研究期间流行病学发现而通过全 基因组 测序事后表征的病毒(人类偏肺病毒 [hMPV]、5 [50·0%] of 10),以及非靶向病毒,例如腺病毒。这些数据有助于鉴定重配 A(H3N2) 流感病毒、潜在的 疫苗 逃逸突变体、流感 抗病毒药物 药物和 RSV 单克隆抗体的易感性标记,以及人类 A(H1N2)v 流感病毒感染。解释:此处显示的跨异质循环病毒的技术上稳健且全面的性能支持在高 患病率 环境中进行更大型前瞻性研究的评估,在这些环境中实施可能是最具成本效益的。基于探针的富集策略的进一步优化可以减少周转时间并促进融入常规公共卫生 监测。资助:荷兰卫生研究与发展组织 (ZonMw) 以及卫生、福利和体育部 (VWS)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过在全国初级保健哨兵 监测 中试点基于探针的宏基因组学来检测和表征预期的和新出现的呼吸道病毒,解决了公共卫生 监测 中成熟宏基因组方法的需求。
设计与对象
2025 年 1 月 10 日至 4 月 25 日,荷兰进行了一项前瞻性试点研究,招募了参加全国前哨病毒学 监测 的全科医生执业的急性呼吸道疾病患者。
核心方法
在预定的选择日,使用针对 15,488 种人类和动物病毒株的探针进行平行宏基因组测试,对 90-100 个鼻咽和口咽拭子样本进行分析,并将结果与​​包括 PCR 和基于扩增子的纳米孔测序的标准两层 监测 策略进行比较。
主要结果
宏基因组学 监测 在 93 份样本中的 88 份 (95%) 中检测到病毒,包括 DNA 病毒,对于常规 PCR 靶向的病毒,宏基因组学显示的汇总敏感性为 93.8% (95% CI 88.1-98.7),特异性为 99.8% (95% CI 99.5-100.0),阳性预测值为 96.2% (95% CI) 91.8-100.0),阴性预测值为 99.7%(95% CI 91.8-100.0),中位 基因组 覆盖率为 99.4%(IQR 96.9-99.9%)。
解释与创新
该研究证明了基于探针的宏基因组学在异质循环病毒中的技术稳健和全面的性能,能够同时对 RSV 和其他病毒进行全基因组表征,包括检测重配 A(H3N2) 流感病毒和人类 A(H1N2)v 流感病毒感染。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在高 患病率 环境下的更大型前瞻性研究中评估基于探针的宏基因组学,以整合到常规公共卫生 监测 中,从而有可能改善呼吸道病毒的基因组表征和检测。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和持续时间短的限制,可能会影响普适性,需要进一步优化基于探针的富集策略,以减少周转时间并提高成本效率。

Probe-based metagenomic sentinel surveillance of viral respiratory infections in primary care: a prospective, national, pilot study.

Authors: Kees Mourik, Igor Sidorov, Erin Meijers, Sharon van den Brink, Sanne Bos, Lynn Aarts, Nithya Kuttiyarthu Veetil, Stefan A Boers, Dirk Eggink, Adam Meijer
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: With the introduction of metagenomics in clinical diagnostics unfolding and the expanding role of pathogen genomics in national surveillance, conditions are favourable for the further maturation of these approaches in public health surveillance. In this study, we aimed to pilot the use of probe-based metagenomics for nationwide sentinel surveillance through general practitioner (GP) networks and for the genomic characterisation of both anticipated and emerging respiratory viruses in primary care. METHODS: This prospective, pilot study included patients with acute respiratory illness attending GP practices participating in nationwide sentinel virological surveillance in the Netherlands, from Jan 10, 2025, to April 25, 2025. On predefined selection days, 90-100 combined nasopharyngeal and oropharyngeal swab specimens were analysed through parallel metagenomic testing using probes targeting 15 488 strains of human and animal viruses. Results were compared with a standard two-tiered surveillance strategy comprising PCR targeting 17 viruses, followed by amplicon-based nanopore whole-genome sequencing of influenza viruses, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). The primary outcome was detection and in-depth genomic characterisation of viruses within and beyond the scope of standard screening. Sensitivity, specificity, positive and negative predictive values, and genome coverage were analysed. FINDINGS: 93 patients were included, with a median age of 51 years (IQR 33-67); 55 (59%) were women and 38 (41%) were men. Overall, 74 (80%) specimens tested positive through standard PCR-based screening. Metagenomic surveillance detected viruses in 88 (95%) specimens, including DNA viruses known to establish latent infections. For viruses targeted by routine PCR, metagenomics showed a pooled sensitivity of 93·8% (95% CI 88·1-98·7), specificity of 99·8% (95% CI 99·5-100·0), positive predictive value of 96·2% (95% CI 91·8-100·0), and negative predictive value of 99·7% (95% CI 91·8-100·0), with a median genome coverage of 99·4% (IQR 96·9-99·9%). Metagenomic data enabled simultaneous full genomic characterisation of circulating viruses targeted by current amplicon-based surveillance, including influenza viruses (success rate 30 [86%] of 35 detections) and RSV (six [86%] of seven), and of viruses that were post hoc characterised by whole-genome sequencing in response to epidemiological findings during the study period (human metapneumoviruses [hMPV], five [50·0%] of ten), and non-targeted viruses such as adenoviruses. The data facilitated characterisation of a reassortant A(H3N2) influenza virus, potential vaccine escape mutants, markers of susceptibility to influenza antiviral drugs and RSV monoclonal antibodies, and a human A(H1N2)v influenza virus infection. INTERPRETATION: The technically robust and comprehensive performance across heterogeneous circulating viruses shown here supports evaluation in larger prospective studies in high-prevalence settings, where implementation might be most cost-efficient. Further optimisation of probe-based enrichment strategies could reduce turnaround time and facilitate integration into routine public health surveillance. FUNDING: The Netherlands Organisation for Health Research and Development (ZonMw), and the Ministry of Health, Welfare and Sport (VWS).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to mature metagenomic approaches in public health surveillance by piloting probe-based metagenomics for nationwide sentinel surveillance in primary care to detect and characterise both anticipated and emerging respiratory viruses.
Design and population
A prospective, pilot study was conducted in the Netherlands from January 10 to April 25, 2025, enrolling patients with acute respiratory illness attending general practitioner practices participating in nationwide sentinel virological surveillance.
Core methods
On predefined selection days, 90-100 combined nasopharyngeal and oropharyngeal swab specimens were analysed using parallel metagenomic testing with probes targeting 15,488 strains of human and animal viruses, and results were compared with a standard two-tiered surveillance strategy comprising PCR and amplicon-based nanopore sequencing.
Main results
Metagenomic surveillance detected viruses in 88 (95%) of 93 specimens, including DNA viruses, and for viruses targeted by routine PCR, metagenomics showed a pooled sensitivity of 93.8% (95% CI 88.1-98.7), specificity of 99.8% (95% CI 99.5-100.0), positive predictive value of 96.2% (95% CI 91.8-100.0), and negative predictive value of 99.7% (95% CI 91.8-100.0), with a median genome coverage of 99.4% (IQR 96.9-99.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated technically robust and comprehensive performance of probe-based metagenomics across heterogeneous circulating viruses, enabling simultaneous full genomic characterisation of RSV and other viruses, including detection of a reassortant A(H3N2) influenza virus and a human A(H1N2)v influenza virus infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support evaluation of probe-based metagenomics in larger prospective studies in high-prevalence settings for integration into routine public health surveillance, potentially improving genomic characterisation and detection of respiratory viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its small sample size and short duration, which may affect generalisability, and further optimisation of probe-based enrichment strategies is needed to reduce turnaround time and enhance cost-efficiency.

快速六病毒抗原检测后成人急性呼吸道感染的抗菌药物处方:意大利初级保健的一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究

Antimicrobial prescribing in adults with acute respiratory infections following rapid six-virus antigen testing: A prospective cohort study in Italian primary care
作者： Andrea Orsi, Alexander Domnich, Antonio Vena, Valentina Ricucci, Matilde Ogliastro, Carlo-Simone Trombetta, Giulia Canepa, Donatella Panatto, Andrea Carraro, Matteo Bassetti
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 过度使用抗生素治疗急性呼吸道感染(ARIs)是一个全球性挑战。虽然多重快速抗原检测试验 (RADT) 已被提出来减少临床不确定性,但有关其对门诊抗菌药物处方影响的证据有限。我们的目的是评估 ARI 成人门诊患者的多重 RADT 结果与抗菌药物处方率之间的关联。方法 这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究是在 2025/2026 呼吸季节期间在意大利热那亚进行的。向 27 名患有 ARI 的全科医生就诊的社区居住成年人(≥ 18 岁)被纳入并进行长达 30 天的随访。在初次访视时,患者接受了两次鼻咽拭子检查。第一个测试是使用针对甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、SARS-CoV-2、偏肺病毒和鼻病毒的多重 RADT 进行测试。第二个拭子被送往实验室,通过标准护理实时聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 进行检测。使用改良泊松回归估计 RADT 阳性和 RADT 阴性患者之间抗菌药物处方的相对风险,并调整潜在的混杂因素。结果 682 名接受 RADT 检测的患者中,18.6% 的病毒检测呈 ≥ 1 种阳性;大多数病例是由甲型流感(7.8%)和呼吸道合胞病毒(5.9%)引起的。与 RT-PCR 相比,RADT 显示出低至中等的敏感性(从鼻病毒的 0.7% 到 SARS-CoV-2 的 77.8%)和高特异性(> 99%)。症状出现 3 天之内收集的样本的敏感性较高。26.0% 的 RADT 阳性患者和 33.9% 的 RADT 阴性患者使用了抗生素,调整后的相对风险为 0.73(95% CI:0.54-0.99)。与 RT-PCR 结果相比,抗生素的使用更多地受到 RADT 结果的驱动:真阴性和假阴性率(34.4% 和 33.5%)相当,超过真阳性率和假阳性率(26.2% 和 20.0%)。所有六个 抗病毒药物 处方均发生在 RADT 阳性个体中。结论 多重 RADT 结果呈阳性与抗生素处方略有减少和 抗病毒药物 使用增加相关。然而,低到中等的敏感性限制了其作为通用排除工具的实用性。多重 RADT 最好作为早期就诊者和高危患者的常规诊断,这些患者将从及时的 抗病毒药物 治疗中受益,而不是作为减少总体抗生素消耗的一般策略。临床试验编号:不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
过度使用抗生素治疗急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 是一项全球性挑战,虽然已提出多重快速抗原检测试验 (RADT) 来减少临床不确定性,但有关其对门诊抗菌药物处方影响的证据有限。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究于 2025/2026 年呼吸季节在意大利热那亚进行,招募了社区居住的成年人(≥ 18 岁),向 27 名患有 ARI 的全科医生就诊,并对他们进行长达 30 天的跟踪。
核心方法
在初次访视时,患者接受了两次鼻咽拭子检测:第一次使用针对甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、SARS-CoV-2、偏肺病毒和鼻病毒的多重 RADT 进行检测,第二次则进行标准护理 RT-PCR 检测。使用改良泊松回归估计 RADT 阳性和 RADT 阴性患者之间抗菌药物处方的相对风险,并调整潜在的混杂因素。
主要结果
在 RADT 检测的 682 名患者中,18.6% 的病毒检测呈 ≥ 1 种阳性,其中大多数病例是甲型流感 (7.8%) 和 RSV (5.9%)。26.0% 的 RADT 阳性患者和 33.9% 的 RADT 阴性患者使用了抗生素,调整后相对风险为 0.73(95% CI:0.54-0.99)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,多重 RADT 结果阳性与抗生素处方略有减少和 抗病毒药物 使用增加相关,这表明多重 RADT 最好作为早期就诊者和高危患者的常规诊断,而不是作为减少总体抗生素消耗的一般策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现谨慎地表明,多重 RADT 可以通过减少 RSV 阳性成人不必要的抗生素处方来支持初级保健中的有针对性的抗菌药物管理,特别是在早期部署于可以从及时 抗病毒药物 治疗中受益的高危患者中时。
局限与证据强度
RADT 的低到中等敏感性,特别是对于 RSV,限制了其作为通用排除工具的实用性,并且该研究的单季节、单区域设计限制了普遍性,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Antimicrobial prescribing in adults with acute respiratory infections following rapid six-virus antigen testing: A prospective cohort study in Italian primary care

Authors: Andrea Orsi, Alexander Domnich, Antonio Vena, Valentina Ricucci, Matilde Ogliastro, Carlo-Simone Trombetta, Giulia Canepa, Donatella Panatto, Andrea Carraro, Matteo Bassetti
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The overuse of antibiotics for acute respiratory infections (ARIs) is a global challenge. While multiplex rapid antigen detection tests (RADTs) have been proposed to reduce clinical uncertainty, evidence regarding their impact on antimicrobial prescribing in outpatient settings is limited. We aimed to evaluate the association between multiplex RADT results and antimicrobial prescription rates in adult outpatients with ARIs. Methods This prospective cohort study was conducted in Genoa, Italy, during the 2025/2026 respiratory season. Community-dwelling adults (≥ 18 years) presenting to 27 general practitioners with an ARI were enrolled and followed for up to 30 days. At the index visit, patients underwent two nasopharyngeal swabs. The first was tested using a multiplex RADT targeting influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), SARS-CoV-2, metapneumovirus, and rhinovirus. The second swab was sent to a laboratory for testing via standard-of-care real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR). The relative risk of antimicrobial prescribing between RADT-positive and RADT-negative patients was estimated using modified Poisson regression, adjusting for potential confounders. Results Of 682 patients tested by RADT, 18.6% tested positive for ≥ 1 virus; most cases were due to influenza A (7.8%) and RSV (5.9%). Compared to RT-PCR, the RADT showed low-to-moderate sensitivity (ranging from 0.7% for rhinovirus to 77.8% for SARS-CoV-2) and high specificity (> 99%). Sensitivity was higher for samples collected ≤ 3 days of symptom onset. Antibiotics were prescribed to 26.0% of RADT-positive and 33.9% of RADT-negative patients, with an adjusted relative risk of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.54–0.99). Antibiotic use was driven more by RADT results than RT-PCR: rates for true and false negatives (34.4% and 33.5%) were comparable, exceeding those for true and false positives (26.2% and 20.0%). All six antiviral prescriptions occurred among RADT-positive individuals. Conclusions A positive multiplex RADT result was associated with slightly reduced antibiotic prescribing and increased antiviral use. However, low-to-moderate sensitivity limits its utility as a universal rule-out tool. Multiplex RADTs could be best deployed as rule-in diagnostics for early presenters and high-risk patients who would benefit from prompt antiviral therapy, rather than as a general strategy to reduce overall antibiotic consumption. Clinical trial number: not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The overuse of antibiotics for acute respiratory infections (ARIs) is a global challenge, and while multiplex rapid antigen detection tests (RADTs) have been proposed to reduce clinical uncertainty, evidence regarding their impact on antimicrobial prescribing in outpatient settings is limited.
Design and population
This prospective cohort study was conducted in Genoa, Italy, during the 2025/2026 respiratory season, enrolling community-dwelling adults (≥ 18 years) presenting to 27 general practitioners with an ARI and following them for up to 30 days.
Core methods
At the index visit, patients underwent two nasopharyngeal swabs: the first tested using a multiplex RADT targeting influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), SARS-CoV-2, metapneumovirus, and rhinovirus, and the second sent for standard-of-care RT-PCR testing. The relative risk of antimicrobial prescribing between RADT-positive and RADT-negative patients was estimated using modified Poisson regression, adjusting for potential confounders.
Main results
Of 682 patients tested by RADT, 18.6% tested positive for ≥ 1 virus, with most cases due to influenza A (7.8%) and RSV (5.9%). Antibiotics were prescribed to 26.0% of RADT-positive and 33.9% of RADT-negative patients, yielding an adjusted relative risk of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.54–0.99).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating that a positive multiplex RADT result was associated with slightly reduced antibiotic prescribing and increased antiviral use, suggesting that multiplex RADTs could be best deployed as rule-in diagnostics for early presenters and high-risk patients rather than as a general strategy to reduce overall antibiotic consumption.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings cautiously inform that multiplex RADTs may support targeted antimicrobial stewardship in primary care by reducing unnecessary antibiotic prescriptions in RSV-positive adults, particularly when deployed early in high-risk patients who could benefit from prompt antiviral therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The low-to-moderate sensitivity of the RADT, especially for RSV, limits its utility as a universal rule-out tool, and the study’s single-season, single-region design constrains generalisability, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

L型钙通道Cav1.2通过调节病毒内化和内质网稳态介导甲型流感病毒感染。

The L-type calcium channel Cav1.2 mediates influenza A virus infection by regulating viral internalization and endoplasmic reticulum homeostasis.
作者： Zhixuan Chen, Ran Luo, Siqi Luo, Hanhui Zhong, Xucheng He, Yinyan Li, Jinxiang Chen, Jie Yang
摘要中文翻译由于 突变 的高发病率和 抗病毒药物 耐药性的出现,甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是全球主要的健康威胁。针对 宿主 因子提供了一种替代治疗策略来克服这些挑战。先前的研究表明,L 型钙通道 Ca v 1.2 与病毒感染有关。在本研究中,我们旨在使用二氢吡啶型钙通道阻滞剂尼索地平阐明 Ca v 1.2 作为介导 IAV 感染的关键 宿主 因子的机制。尼索地平在体外和体内模型中均显示出显着的 抗病毒药物 活性。从机制上讲,尼索地平通过靶向 Ca v 1.2 抑制 IAV 内化,从而抑制与病毒内化相关的 Ras-PI3K-AKT、RhoA-ROCK 和 JNK 通路的钙依赖性激活。此外,尼索地平通过促进适应性 XBP1 剪接调节 宿主 细胞中的未折叠蛋白反应 (UPR),并有助于维持 IAV 感染期间内质网 (ER) 稳态。总的来说,这些观察结果支持顺序级联模型:Ca v 1.2 抑制抑制病毒进入并间接减轻病毒触发的内质网应激。此外,尼索地平对多种有包膜 RNA 和 DNA 病毒表现出广谱体外 抗病毒药物 活性,包括人类冠状病毒 OC43 (HCoV-OC43)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、登革热病毒 2 型 (DENV2) 和单纯疱疹病毒 1/2 型 (HSV-1/2)。这项研究阐明了 Ca v 1.2 在 IAV 感染中的作用机制,并进一步将其确定为一种有前景的广谱 抗病毒药物 靶点,在预防和治疗流感、人类冠状病毒感染和其他新出现的病毒性疾病方面具有潜在应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 由于其高 突变 发生率和 抗病毒药物 耐药性,对全球健康构成持续威胁,因此需要替代治疗策略。针对 宿主 因子(例如 L 型钙通道 Cav1.2)提供了克服这些挑战的潜在方法。
设计与对象
该研究使用钙通道阻滞剂尼索地平在体外和体内模型中研究了 Cav1.2 在 IAV 感染中的作用。
核心方法
作者使用尼索地平抑制 Cav1.2,并检查了其对 IAV 内化和 宿主 细胞信号通路的影响,包括 Ras-PI3K-AKT、RhoA-ROCK 和 JNK,以及未折叠蛋白反应 (UPR) 和内质网 (ER) 稳态。
主要结果
尼索地平通过 Cav1.2 靶向抑制 IAV 内化,在体外和体内模型中表现出显着的 抗病毒药物 活性。
解释与创新
该研究阐明,Cav1.2 抑制可抑制病毒进入并减轻病毒触发的内质网应激,支持 IAV 感染的顺序级联模型。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Cav1.2 是一个有前途的广谱 抗病毒药物 靶点,在预防和治疗流感和其他新出现的病毒性疾病方面具有潜在的应用前景。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告研究局限性、样本量或统计分析,这限制了结论的强度。

The L-type calcium channel Cav1.2 mediates influenza A virus infection by regulating viral internalization and endoplasmic reticulum homeostasis.

Authors: Zhixuan Chen, Ran Luo, Siqi Luo, Hanhui Zhong, Xucheng He, Yinyan Li, Jinxiang Chen, Jie Yang
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a major global health threat due to its high mutation rate and the emergence of antiviral resistance. Targeting host factors provides an alternative therapeutic strategy to overcome these challenges. Previous studies have shown that the L-type calcium channel Ca v 1.2 was implicated in viral infections. In this study, we aimed to clarify the mechanism by which Ca v 1.2 acts as a critical host factor mediating IAV infection, using the dihydropyridine-type calcium channel blocker nisoldipine. Nisoldipine showed notable antiviral activity in both in vitro and in vivo models. Mechanistically, nisoldipine inhibited IAV internalization by targeting Ca v 1.2, thereby suppressing calcium-dependent activation of the Ras-PI3K-AKT, RhoA-ROCK, and JNK pathways associated with viral internalization. Moreover, nisoldipine modulated the unfolded protein response (UPR) in host cells by promoting adaptive XBP1 splicing and contributed to the maintenance of endoplasmic reticulum (ER) homeostasis during IAV infection. Collectively, these observations support a sequential cascade model: Ca v 1.2 inhibition restrains viral entry and indirectly mitigates virus-triggered ER stress. Furthermore, nisoldipine exhibits broad-spectrum in vitro antiviral activity against multiple enveloped RNA and DNA viruses, including human coronavirus OC43 (HCoV-OC43), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Dengue virus Type 2 (DENV2), and Herpes Simplex Virus Type 1/2 (HSV-1/2). This study clarifies the mechanistic involvement of Ca v 1.2 in IAV infection and further identifies it as a promising broad-spectrum antiviral target with potential applications in preventing and treating influenza, human coronavirus infections, and other emerging viral diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus (IAV) poses a persistent global health threat due to its high mutation rate and antiviral resistance, creating a need for alternative therapeutic strategies. Targeting host factors such as the L-type calcium channel Cav1.2 offers a potential approach to overcome these challenges.
Design and population
The study investigated the role of Cav1.2 in IAV infection using the calcium channel blocker nisoldipine across in vitro and in vivo models.
Core methods
The authors used nisoldipine to inhibit Cav1.2 and examined its effects on IAV internalization and host cell signaling pathways, including Ras-PI3K-AKT, RhoA-ROCK, and JNK, as well as the unfolded protein response (UPR) and endoplasmic reticulum (ER) homeostasis.
Main results
Nisoldipine demonstrated notable antiviral activity in both in vitro and in vivo models by inhibiting IAV internalization through Cav1.2 targeting.
Interpretation and novelty
The study clarifies that Cav1.2 inhibition restrains viral entry and mitigates virus-triggered ER stress, supporting a sequential cascade model of IAV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings identify Cav1.2 as a promising broad-spectrum antiviral target with potential applications in preventing and treating influenza and other emerging viral diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations, sample sizes, or statistical analyses, which limits the strength of the conclusions.

使用目标试验模拟 尼塞韦单抗 对儿童抗生素使用的有效性。

The Effectiveness of Nirsevimab on Antibiotic Use in Children Using Target Trial Emulation.
作者： Mahaa M Ahmed, Torsten A. Joerger, Ziyi Wang, Yun Li, Jeffrey S Gerber
摘要中文翻译背景:尼塞韦单抗 是一种单克隆抗体,已被证实可有效预防婴儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染。由于急性呼吸道感染 (ARTI),包括病毒感染,通常会导致开抗生素处方,因此我们旨在确定 尼塞韦单抗 对患有 ARTI 的婴儿开抗生素处方的有效性。方法:使用来自医疗保健网络 32 个不同诊所的电子健康记录数据建立了初级保健参与者 队列研究,以模拟一项目标试验,该试验在 8 个月以下的婴儿中比较 尼塞韦单抗 和无 治疗。符合条件的婴儿在出生后 14 天内到初级保健诊所就诊。主要结局包括 (1) 门诊 ARTI 首次处方抗生素处方的时间,(2) 毛细支气管炎 门诊就诊情况,以及 (3) RSV 相关住院治疗。尼塞韦单抗 有效性的计算方法为 1 减去每个主要结局的风险比。结果:在 15 341 例符合纳入标准的患者中,7 413 例接受了 尼塞韦单抗。与不使用 治疗 相比,尼塞韦单抗 给药导致门诊 ARTI 的抗生素处方减少 14.4%(95% CI:7.8%,20.6%),门诊 毛细支气管炎 的抗生素处方减少 40.3%(95% CI:25.1%,52.5%),以及 69.4% (95% CI: 4.8%,90.1%) RSV 相关住院抗生素处方减少。结论:初级保健中的 尼塞韦单抗 给药减少了门诊和住院环境中 ARTI 抗生素的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尼塞韦单抗 是一种单克隆抗体,已被证明可有效预防婴儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染。由于急性呼吸道感染 (ARTI),包括病毒感染,通常会导致开抗生素处方,因此我们旨在确定 尼塞韦单抗 对患有 ARTI 的婴儿开抗生素处方的有效性。
设计与对象
使用来自医疗保健网络中 32 个不同诊所的电子健康记录数据建立了初级保健参与者 队列研究,以模拟一项目标试验,该试验在 8 个月以下的婴儿中比较 尼塞韦单抗 和无 治疗。符合条件的婴儿在出生后 14 天内到初级保健诊所就诊。
核心方法
主要结局包括 (1) 门诊 ARTI 首次处方抗生素处方的时间,(2) 毛细支气管炎 门诊就诊情况,以及 (3) RSV 相关住院治疗。尼塞韦单抗 有效性的计算方法为 1 减去每个主要结局的风险比。
主要结果
在 15341 名符合纳入标准的患者中,7413 名患者接受了 尼塞韦单抗 治疗。与不使用 治疗 相比,尼塞韦单抗 给药导致门诊 ARTI 的抗生素处方减少 14.4%(95% CI:7.8%,20.6%),门诊 毛细支气管炎 的抗生素处方减少 40.3%(95% CI:25.1%,52.5%),以及 69.4% (95% CI: 4.8%,90.1%) RSV 相关住院抗生素处方减少。
解释与创新
初级保健中的 尼塞韦单抗 给药减少了门诊和住院环境中 ARTI 抗生素的使用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,尼塞韦单抗 可以减少 RSV 相关 ARTI 中不必要的抗生素使用,有可能减轻抗生素耐药性并改善婴儿的临床结果。
局限与证据强度
研究设计基于电子健康记录数据,这可能会引入偏差和混杂因素。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

The Effectiveness of Nirsevimab on Antibiotic Use in Children Using Target Trial Emulation.

Authors: Mahaa M Ahmed, Torsten A. Joerger, Ziyi Wang, Yun Li, Jeffrey S Gerber
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Nirsevimab is a monoclonal antibody with demonstrated efficacy in preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infections in infants. Because acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs), including viral infections, often lead to antibiotic prescribing, we aimed to determine the effectiveness of nirsevimab on antibiotic prescribing among infants with medically attended ARTIs. METHODS: A primary care attendee cohort was established using electronic health record data from 32 diverse practices across a healthcare network to emulate a target trial comparing nirsevimab to no treatment among infants <8 months. Eligible infants were seen at a primary care practice within 14 days of life. Primary outcomes included time to first antibiotic prescription for (1) outpatient ARTIs, (2) outpatient encounters for bronchiolitis, and (3) RSV-related hospitalizations. Nirsevimab effectiveness was calculated as 1 minus the risk ratio for each primary outcome. RESULTS: Among 15 341 patients who met inclusion criteria, 7413 received nirsevimab. Compared with no treatment, nirsevimab administration led to a 14.4% (95% CI: 7.8%, 20.6%) reduction in antibiotic prescribing for outpatient ARTIs, a 40.3% (95% CI: 25.1%, 52.5%) reduction in antibiotic prescribing for outpatient bronchiolitis, and a 69.4% (95% CI: 4.8%, 90.1%) reduction in antibiotic prescribing for RSV-related hospitalizations. CONCLUSIONS: Nirsevimab administration in primary care reduced antibiotic use for ARTIs in both ambulatory and inpatient settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nirsevimab is a monoclonal antibody with demonstrated efficacy in preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infections in infants. Because acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs), including viral infections, often lead to antibiotic prescribing, we aimed to determine the effectiveness of nirsevimab on antibiotic prescribing among infants with medically attended ARTIs.
Design and population
A primary care attendee cohort was established using electronic health record data from 32 diverse practices across a healthcare network to emulate a target trial comparing nirsevimab to no treatment among infants <8 months. Eligible infants were seen at a primary care practice within 14 days of life.
Core methods
Primary outcomes included time to first antibiotic prescription for (1) outpatient ARTIs, (2) outpatient encounters for bronchiolitis, and (3) RSV-related hospitalizations. Nirsevimab effectiveness was calculated as 1 minus the risk ratio for each primary outcome.
Main results
Among 15 341 patients who met inclusion criteria, 7413 received nirsevimab. Compared with no treatment, nirsevimab administration led to a 14.4% (95% CI: 7.8%, 20.6%) reduction in antibiotic prescribing for outpatient ARTIs, a 40.3% (95% CI: 25.1%, 52.5%) reduction in antibiotic prescribing for outpatient bronchiolitis, and a 69.4% (95% CI: 4.8%, 90.1%) reduction in antibiotic prescribing for RSV-related hospitalizations.
Interpretation and novelty
Nirsevimab administration in primary care reduced antibiotic use for ARTIs in both ambulatory and inpatient settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nirsevimab could reduce unnecessary antibiotic use for RSV-related ARTIs, potentially mitigating antibiotic resistance and improving clinical outcomes for infants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design is based on electronic health record data, which may introduce biases and confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 对爱尔兰岛婴儿住院治疗的有效性。

Effectiveness of nirsevimab and maternal RSVpreF vaccination against infant hospitalisation across the island of Ireland.
作者： B Treston, S O'Hagan, M Barrett, R Marlow, T C Williams, M Lyttle, D Roland, A Walsh, T Waterfield, S Geoghegan
摘要中文翻译目标:2024 年,爱尔兰岛引入了两种 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫策略:爱尔兰共和国 (ROI) 的 尼塞韦单抗 和北爱尔兰 (NI) 的 RSVpreF。主要目的是评估 6 个月以下婴儿住院 RSV 相关急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRI) 的有效性。第二个目标是比较对急诊科 (ED) 毛细支气管炎 出勤率的影响。方法:我们在 2024/25 赛季期间在五个地点进行了一项多中心、测试阴性、病例对照研究。使用多变量逻辑回归调整年龄 <3 个月、性别和早产来估计住院治疗的有效性。2024 年 9 月 1 日后出生、因 ALRI 住院并接受 RSV 检测的婴儿符合资格。时间序列模型将研究季节期间 ED 毛细支气管炎 的出勤率与前五个季节进行了比较。结果:纳入 254 名婴儿(ROI:165,NI:89)。在 ROI 中,34% 的病例和 83% 的对照接受了 尼塞韦单抗;在北卡罗来纳州,分别有 6% 和 30% 的人接受了 RSVpreF。尼塞韦单抗 的调整后住院有效性为 90.7% (95% CI 80.7-95.8),RSVpreF 的调整后有效性为 83.9% (95% CI 41.5-96.6)。模型显示 ED 毛细支气管炎 就诊人数显着减少;投资回报率 (52%) 高于 NI (37%)。结论:两种干预措施对于 RSV-ALRI 住院治疗均非常有效。然而,覆盖范围存在显着差异,这对人口层面的影响具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估爱尔兰岛于 2024 年推出的两种 RSV 免疫策略对婴儿因 RSV 相关急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRI) 住院的有效性。
设计与对象
2024/25 赛季期间,在五个地点进行了一项多中心、检测呈阴性的病例对照研究,其中包括 2024 年 9 月 1 日之后出生、因 ALRI 住院并接受 RSV 检测的婴儿。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归调整年龄 <3 个月、性别和早产来估计住院治疗的有效性。使用时间序列模型将研究季节期间 ED 毛细支气管炎 的出勤率与之前五个季节进行比较。
主要结果
纳入 254 名婴儿(ROI:165,NI:89)。尼塞韦单抗 的调整后住院有效性为 90.7% (95% CI 80.7-95.8),RSVpreF 的调整后有效性为 83.9% (95% CI 41.5-96.6)。模型显示 ED 毛细支气管炎 就诊人数显着减少;投资回报率 (52%) 高于 NI (37%)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,这两种干预措施对于 RSV-ALRI 住院治疗都非常有效,覆盖范围存在显着差异,这对人口层面的影响具有重要意义。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为了解 RSV 疫苗 在减少婴儿 毛细支气管炎 住院和急诊就诊率方面的有效性提供了重要见解,这可以为公共卫生策略和 疫苗 的实施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其仅抽象分析的设计而受到限制,并且由于可能存在混杂因素以及对观察数据的依赖,证据强度适中。

Effectiveness of nirsevimab and maternal RSVpreF vaccination against infant hospitalisation across the island of Ireland.

Authors: B Treston, S O'Hagan, M Barrett, R Marlow, T C Williams, M Lyttle, D Roland, A Walsh, T Waterfield, S Geoghegan
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: In 2024, two respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunisation strategies were introduced on the island of Ireland: nirsevimab in Republic of Ireland (ROI) and RSVpreF in Northern Ireland (NI). The primary objective was to estimate effectiveness against hospitalised RSV-associated acute lower respiratory infection (ALRI) in infants <6 months. The secondary objective was to compare impact on emergency department (ED) bronchiolitis attendances. METHODS: We conducted a multicentre, test-negative, case-control study across five sites during the 2024/25 season. Effectiveness against hospitalisation was estimated using multivariable logistic regression adjusting for age <3 months, sex, and prematurity. Infants born after 1 September 2024, hospitalised with ALRI, and tested for RSV were eligible. Time series modelling compared ED bronchiolitis attendances during the study season with five preceding seasons. RESULTS: 254 infants were included (ROI:165, NI: 89). In ROI, 34% of cases and 83% of controls received nirsevimab; in NI, 6% and 30% received RSVpreF respectively. Adjusted effectiveness against hospitalisation was 90.7% (95% CI 80.7-95.8) for nirsevimab and 83.9% (95% CI 41.5-96.6) for RSVpreF. Modelling showed significant reductions in ED bronchiolitis attendances; greater in ROI (52%) than NI (37%). CONCLUSIONS: Both interventions were highly effective against RSV-ALRI hospitalisation. However, coverage differed markedly, with important implications for population-level impact.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of two RSV immunisation strategies introduced in 2024 on the island of Ireland against infant hospitalisation for RSV-associated acute lower respiratory infection (ALRI).
Design and population
A multicentre, test-negative, case-control study was conducted across five sites during the 2024/25 season, including infants born after 1 September 2024 who were hospitalised with ALRI and tested for RSV.
Core methods
Effectiveness against hospitalisation was estimated using multivariable logistic regression adjusting for age <3 months, sex, and prematurity. Time series modelling was used to compare ED bronchiolitis attendances during the study season with five preceding seasons.
Main results
254 infants were included (ROI:165, NI: 89). Adjusted effectiveness against hospitalisation was 90.7% (95% CI 80.7-95.8) for nirsevimab and 83.9% (95% CI 41.5-96.6) for RSVpreF. Modelling showed significant reductions in ED bronchiolitis attendances; greater in ROI (52%) than NI (37%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that both interventions were highly effective against RSV-ALRI hospitalisation, with notable differences in coverage that have important implications for population-level impact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide important insights into the effectiveness of RSV vaccines in reducing hospitalisations and ED attendances for bronchiolitis in infants, which can inform public health strategies and vaccine implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its design as an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the potential for confounding factors and the reliance on observational data.
